  const renamePlaylist = useMutation(api.admin.renamePlaylist);
  const clearUserHistory = useMutation(api.admin.clearUserHistory);
  const deleteRoom = useMutation(api.admin.deleteRoom);
  const deleteImport = useMutation(api.admin.deleteImport);
  const fetchGithub = useAction(api.admin.githubProfile);

  const listeningStats = useQuery(api.admin.platformListeningStats, {});
  const recentSignups = useQuery(api.admin.recentSignups, {});
  const rooms = useQuery(api.admin.listRooms, {});
  const imports = useQuery(api.admin.listImports, {});

  const [renaming, setRenaming] = useState<{
    id: Id<\"playlists\">;
    name: string;
  } | null>(null);

  const handleRenamePlaylist = async (
    playlistId: Id<\"playlists\">,
    name: string,
  ) => {
    try {
      await renamePlaylist({ playlistId, name });
      toast.success(\"Playlist renamed\");
      setRenaming(null);
    } catch {
      toast.error(\"Could not rename the playlist\");
    }
  };

  const handleClearHistory = async (userId: Id<\"users\">, name: string) => {
    try {
      const n = await clearUserHistory({ userId });
      toast.success(`Cleared ${n} history rows for ${name}`);
    } catch {
      toast.error(\"Could not clear the history\");
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteRoom = async (roomId: Id<\"listeningRooms\">) => {
    try {
      await deleteRoom({ roomId });
      toast.success(\"Listening room removed\");
    } catch {
      toast.error(\"Could not remove the room\");
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteImport = async (importId: Id<\"importedPlaylists\">) => {
    try {
      await deleteImport({ importId });
      toast.success(\"Import deleted\");
    } catch {
      toast.error(\"Could not delete the import\");
    }
  };

  const [tab, setTab] = useState(\"overview\");
  // Table controls: instant client-side narrowing so admins don't have to page
  // through long lists, and so they work without extra round trips on mobile.
  const [userQuery, setUserQuery] = useState(\"\");
  const [userRole, setUserRole] = useState<\"all\" | \"admin\" | \"user\">(\"all\");
  const [userMethod, setUserMethod] = useState<UserMethodFilter>(\"all\");
  const [userSort, setUserSort] = useState<\"newest\" | \"oldest\" | \"name\">(
    \"newest\",
  );
  const [playlistQuery, setPlaylistQuery] = useState(\"\");
  const [playlistSort, setPlaylistSort] = useState<
    \"newest\" | \"largest\" | \"name\"
  >(\"newest\");
  const [globalQuery, setGlobalQuery] = useState(\"\");

  /**
   * Which playlist an admin has open for inspection, if any.
   *
   * The playlists table showed a row per playlist with a track COUNT, but
   * there was no way to see the contents. Handling a report or a copyright
   * complaint needs the actual tracks, and the only way to get them was to ask
   * the user to screenshot their playlist.
   */
  const [inspecting, setInspecting] = useState<Id<\"playlists\"> | null>(null);
  const inspected = useQuery(
    api.admin.playlistDetail,
    inspecting ? { playlistId: inspecting } : \"skip\",
  );

  const filteredUsers = useMemo(() => {
    const q = userQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    const rows = (users ?? []).filter((u) => {
      if (userRole === \"admin\" && u.role !== \"admin\") return false;
      if (userRole === \"user\" && u.role === \"admin\") return false;
      // Narrow by sign-in method: \"who signed up with GitHub\" is the question
      // admins ask most, and the per-row badges can't answer it once the
      // accounts table is long. \"Any sign-in\" clears the filter entirely;
      // \"Other\" catches providers the named methods don't cover, so no
      // account can become invisible.
      if (!userMatchesMethod(u.providers, userMethod)) return false;
      if (!q) return true;
      const name = (u.name ?? \"\").toLowerCase();
      const email = (u.email ?? \"\").toLowerCase();
      // The external GitHub account id is searchable for the same reason the
      // badge shows it: looking it up should land on that row.
      const github = (u.githubId ?? \"\").toLowerCase();
      return (
        name.includes(q) ||
        email.includes(q) ||
        github.includes(q) ||
        u._id.includes(q)
      );
    });
    return rows.sort((a, b) => {
      if (userSort === \"name\")
        return (a.name ?? \"\").localeCompare(b.name ?? \"\");
      if (userSort === \"oldest\") return a._creationTime - b._creationTime;
      return b._creationTime - a._creationTime;
    });
  }, [users, userQuery, userRole, userMethod, userSort]);

  /** How many accounts use each sign-in method, shown on the filter labels. */
  const methodCounts = useMemo(() => {
    // `all` is the \"Any sign-in\" total; `other` counts accounts carrying at
    // least one provider the named methods don't cover, so the numbers add up
    // instead of leaving rows unexplained.
    const counts: Record<string, number> = {
      all: 0,
      [OTHER_SIGN_IN_METHOD]: 0,
    };
    for (const u of (users ?? []) as any[]) {
      counts.all += 1;
      // `providers` carries one entry per linked auth account, so a user with
      // two GitHub credentials would otherwise be counted twice and the label
      // would promise more rows than the filter can show.
      const unique = new Set((u.providers ?? []) as string[]);
      for (const p of unique) {
        if (KNOWN_SIGN_IN_METHODS.has(p)) counts[p] = (counts[p] ?? 0) + 1;
        else counts[OTHER_SIGN_IN_METHOD] += 1;
      }
    }
    return counts;
  }, [users]);

  const filteredPlaylists = useMemo(() => {
    const q = playlistQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    const rows = (playlists ?? []).filter(
      (p) =>
        !q ||
        p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        p._id.includes(q) ||
        (p.ownerName ?? \"\").toLowerCase().includes(q),
    );
    return rows.sort((a, b) => {
      if (playlistSort === \"largest\") return b.trackCount - a.trackCount;
      if (playlistSort === \"name\") return a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
      return b._creationTime - a._creationTime;
    });
  }, [playlists, playlistQuery, playlistSort]);

  /** Cross-entity search over the data already loaded for the admin. */
  const globalResults = useMemo(() => {
    const q = globalQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return null;
    return {
      users: (users ?? [])
        .filter(
          (u) =>
            (u.name ?? \"\").toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            (u.email ?? \"\").toLowerCase().includes(q),
        )
        .slice(0, 25),
      playlists: (playlists ?? [])
        .filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || p._id.includes(q))
        .slice(0, 25),
      likes: (allLikes ?? [])
        .filter(
          (l) =>
            l.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            l.artistName.toLowerCase().includes(q),
        )
        .slice(0, 25),
      rooms: (rooms ?? [])
        .filter((r) => (r.name ?? \"\").toLowerCase().includes(q))
        .slice(0, 25),
      imports: (imports ?? [])
        .filter((i) => (i.name ?? \"\").toLowerCase().includes(q))
        .slice(0, 25),
    };
  }, [globalQuery, users, playlists, allLikes, rooms, imports]);
  const [gh, setGh] = useState<{
    userId: string;
    name: string;
    loading: boolean;
    data: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof fetchGithub>>;
  } | null>(null);

  const openGithub = async (
    userId: string,
    githubId: string | null,
    name: string,
  ) => {
    setGh({ userId, name, loading: true, data: null });
    try {
      const data = githubId ? await fetchGithub({ githubId }) : null;
      setGh({ userId, name, loading: false, data });
    } catch {
      setGh({ userId, name, loading: false, data: null });
    }
  };

  if (status === undefined) {
    return (
      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-3 py-10\">
        <div className=\"h-9 w-64 animate-pulse rounded bg-white/[0.05]\" />
        <div className=\"grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4\">
          {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
            <div
              key={i}
              className=\"h-28 animate-pulse rounded-2xl bg-white/[0.04]\"
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (status === null) {
    return (
      <div className=\"flex flex-col items-center gap-4 py-24 text-center\">
        <span className=\"flex size-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white/[0.05] ring-1 ring-white/10\">
          <ShieldCheck className=\"size-7 text-muted-foreground/60\" />
        </span>
        <div>
          <h1 className=\"font-display text-xl font-bold tracking-tight\">
            Admin access required
          </h1>
          <p className=\"mx-auto mt-1 max-w-sm text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground\">
            This area is reserved for administrators. If you manage this
            deployment, contact an existing admin to be granted access.
          </p>
        </div>
        <Link
          to=\"/home\"
          className=\"mt-2 rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04] px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold transition-colors hover:bg-white/[0.08]\"
        >
          Back to your dashboard
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const counts = status.counts;

  const handleSetRole = async (
    userId: Id<\"users\">,
    name: string,
    role: \"admin\" | \"user\",
  ) => {
    try {
      await setRole({ userId, role });
      toast.success(
        role === \"admin\"
          ? `${name} is now an admin`
          : `${name} is now a standard user`,
      );
    } catch {
      toast.error(\"Could not update the role\");
    }
  };

  const handleDeleteUser = async (userId: Id<\"users\">, name: string) => {
    try {
      await deleteUser({ userId });
      toast.success(`Deleted ${name}'s account`);
    } catch {
      toast.error(\"Could not delete the account\");
    }
  };

  const handleDeletePlaylist = async (playlistId: Id<\"playlists\">) => {
    try {
      await deletePlaylist({ playlistId });
      toast.success(\"Playlist deleted\");
    } catch {
      toast.error(\"Could not delete the playlist\");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-8\">
      <header>
        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">
          <span className=\"flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[rgb(var(--tint)_/_0.14)] text-[rgb(var(--tint))]\">
            <Shield className=\"size-5\" />
          </span>
          <div>
            <h1 className=\"font-display text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-3xl\">
              Admin
            </h1>
            <p className=\"mt-0.5 text-sm text-muted-foreground\">
              Manage accounts, content, and listening activity across the
              deployment.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      <Tabs value={tab} onValueChange={setTab}>
        {/* 7 tabs wrapped into a tall stack on a phone. Scroll the rail
            sideways instead so the admin area stays one-handed. */}
        <TabsList className=\"slider-rail h-auto w-full justify-start gap-1 overflow-x-auto bg-white/[0.05] p-1\">
          <TabsTrigger value=\"overview\" className=\"shrink-0\">
            Overview
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value=\"users\" className=\"shrink-0\">
            Users
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value=\"insights\" className=\"shrink-0\">
            Insights
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value=\"visits\" className=\"shrink-0\">
            Visits
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value=\"likes\" className=\"shrink-0\">
            Liked songs
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value=\"playlists\" className=\"shrink-0\">
            Playlists
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value=\"rooms\" className=\"shrink-0\">
            Listening rooms
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value=\"imports\" className=\"shrink-0\">
            Imports
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value=\"search\" className=\"shrink-0\">
            Search
          </TabsTrigger>
        </TabsList>

        <TabsContent value=\"overview\" className=\"mt-6 flex flex-col gap-6\">
          <div className=\"grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4\">
            <StatCard icon={Users} label=\"Accounts\" value={counts.users} />
            <StatCard icon={ShieldCheck} label=\"Admins\" value={counts.admins} />
            <StatCard
              icon={ListMusic}
              label=\"Playlists\"
              value={counts.playlists}
            />
            <StatCard icon={Heart} label=\"Liked songs\" value={counts.likes} />
          </div>

          {recentSignups !== undefined && recentSignups.length > 0 && (
            <div className=\"rounded-2xl border border-white/[0.07] bg-white/[0.03] p-5\">
              <p className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold\">
                <Sparkles className=\"size-4 text-[rgb(var(--tint))]\" />
                Recent signups
              </p>
              <div className=\"mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2\">
                {recentSignups.map((u) => (
                  <div
                    key={u._id}
                    className=\"flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm\"
                  >
                    <span className=\"min-w-0 truncate\">
                      {u.name ??
                        (u.isAnonymous ? \"Guest listener\" : \"Unnamed listener\")}
                      {u.role === \"admin\" && (
                        <span className=\"ml-2 text-xs text-[rgb(var(--tint))]\">
                          admin
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </span>
                    <span className=\"shrink-0 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">
                      {formatDate(u._creationTime)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className=\"rounded-2xl border border-white/[0.07] bg-white/[0.03] p-5\">
            <p className=\"text-sm font-semibold\">About this admin area</p>
            <p className=\"mt-1.5 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-muted-foreground\">
              From here you can review every account on the deployment, grant or
              revoke admin access, and moderate any playlist. The first account
              ever created on this deployment was automatically promoted to
              admin, which is how you&apos;re here.
            </p>
          </div>
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value=\"search\" className=\"mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3\">
          <input
            value={globalQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setGlobalQuery(e.target.value)}
            placeholder=\"Search accounts, playlists, liked songs, rooms and imports…\"
            aria-label=\"Search everything\"
            className=\"h-11 w-full rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04] px-4 text-sm outline-none placeholder:text-muted-foreground/70 focus:border-[rgb(var(--tint)_/_0.5)]\"
          />
          {!globalResults ? (
            <p className=\"py-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground\">
              Type to search across every list in this admin area.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-4\">
              {globalResults.users.length > 0 && (
                <ResultGroup
                  title=\"Accounts\"
                  count={globalResults.users.length}
                >
                  {globalResults.users.map((u) => (
                    <ResultRow
                      key={u._id}
                      title={
                        u.name ??
                        (u.isAnonymous ? \"Guest listener\" : \"Unnamed listener\")
                      }
                      detail={u.email ?? \"Anonymous sign-in\"}
                    />
                  ))}
                </ResultGroup>
              )}
              {globalResults.playlists.length > 0 && (
                <ResultGroup
                  title=\"Playlists\"
                  count={globalResults.playlists.length}
                >
                  {globalResults.playlists.map((p) => (
                    <ResultRow
                      key={p._id}
                      title={p.name}
                      detail={`${p.trackCount} tracks · ${p.ownerName ?? \"unknown owner\"}`}
                    />
                  ))}
                </ResultGroup>
              )}
              {globalResults.likes.length > 0 && (
                <ResultGroup
                  title=\"Liked songs\"
                  count={globalResults.likes.length}
                >
                  {globalResults.likes.map((l) => (
                    <ResultRow
                      key={`${l._id}`}
                      title={l.title}
                      detail={l.artistName}
                    />
                  ))}
                </ResultGroup>
              )}
              {globalResults.rooms.length > 0 && (
                <ResultGroup
                  title=\"Listening rooms\"
                  count={globalResults.rooms.length}
                >
                  {globalResults.rooms.map((r) => (
                    <ResultRow
                      key={r._id}
                      title={r.name}
                      detail={`${r.memberCount} listeners · ${r.hostName}`}
                    />
                  ))}
                </ResultGroup>
              )}
              {globalResults.imports.length > 0 && (
                <ResultGroup
                  title=\"Imports\"
                  count={globalResults.imports.length}
                >
                  {globalResults.imports.map((i) => (
                    <ResultRow
                      key={i._id}
                      title={i.name ?? \"Untitled import\"}
                      detail={`${i.trackCount ?? 0} tracks`}
                    />
                  ))}
                </ResultGroup>
              )}
              {globalResults.users.length === 0 &&
                globalResults.playlists.length === 0 &&
                globalResults.likes.length === 0 &&
                globalResults.rooms.length === 0 &&
                globalResults.imports.length === 0 && (
                  <p className=\"py-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground\">
                    Nothing matched “{globalQuery}”.
                  </p>
                )}
            </div>
          )}
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value=\"users\" className=\"mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3\">
          <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-center\">
            <input
              value={userQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setUserQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder=\"Search name, email or GitHub id…\"
              aria-label=\"Search accounts\"
              className=\"h-10 w-full rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04] px-4 text-sm outline-none placeholder:text-muted-foreground/70 focus:border-[rgb(var(--tint)_/_0.5)] sm:max-w-xs\"
            />
            <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5\">
              {([\"all\", \"admin\", \"user\"] as const).map((role) => (
                <button
                  key={role}
                  type=\"button\"
                  onClick={() => setUserRole(role)}
                  className={cn(
                    \"rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold capitalize transition-colors\",
                    userRole === role
                      ? \"border-[rgb(var(--tint))] bg-[rgb(var(--tint)_/_0.14)] text-[rgb(var(--tint))]\"
                      : \"border-white/10 text-muted-foreground hover:border-white/25 hover:text-foreground\",
                  )}
                >
                  {role}
                </button>
              ))}
              <select
                value={userMethod}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setUserMethod(e.target.value as typeof userMethod)
                }
                aria-label=\"Filter by sign-in method\"
                className=\"rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04] px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground outline-none\"
              >
                <option value=\"all\">
                  Any sign-in ({methodCounts.all ?? 0})
                </option>
                {SIGN_IN_METHODS.map((method) => (
                  <option key={method.id} value={method.id}>
                    {method.label} ({methodCounts[method.id] ?? 0})
                  </option>
                ))}
                {(methodCounts[OTHER_SIGN_IN_METHOD] ?? 0) > 0 && (
                  <option value={OTHER_SIGN_IN_METHOD}>
                    Other ({methodCounts[OTHER_SIGN_IN_METHOD] ?? 0})
                  </option>
                )}
              </select>
              <select
                value={userSort}
                onChange={(e) => setUserSort(e.target.value as typeof userSort)}
                aria-label=\"Sort accounts\"
                className=\"rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04] px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground outline-none\"
              >
                <option value=\"newest\">Newest</option>
                <option value=\"oldest\">Oldest</option>
                <option value=\"name\">Name</option>
              </select>
              <span className=\"text-[11px] text-muted-foreground\">
                {filteredUsers.length} of {users?.length ?? 0}
              </span>
              <button
                type=\"button\"
                onClick={() => {
                  // Exports exactly what the table is showing, so the active
                  // search / role / sign-in filters carry into the file.
                  const stamp = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
                  const ok = downloadCsv(
                    `synq-accounts-${stamp}.csv`,
                    toCsv(filteredUsers as any[], [
                      { header: \"Name\", value: (u: any) => u.name ?? \"\" },
                      { header: \"Email\", value: (u: any) => u.email ?? \"\" },
                      { header: \"Role\", value: (u: any) => u.role ?? \"user\" },
                      {
                        header: \"Sign-in\",
                        value: (u: any) => (u.providers ?? []).join(\" + \"),
                      },
                      { header: \"GitHub id\", value: (u: any) => u.githubId ?? \"\" },
                      {
                        header: \"Guest\",
                        value: (u: any) => (u.isAnonymous ? \"yes\" : \"no\"),
                      },
                      {
                        header: \"Joined\",
                        value: (u: any) => new Date(u._creationTime).toISOString(),
                      },
                      { header: \"Account id\", value: (u: any) => u._id },
                    ]),
                  );
                  if (!ok) {
                    toast.error(\"Couldn't export the accounts\");
                    return;
                  }
                  toast.success(
                    `Exported ${filteredUsers.length} account${filteredUsers.length === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"}`,
                  );
                }}
                className=\"flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:border-white/25 hover:text-foreground\"
              >
                <ArrowDownToLine className=\"size-3.5\" />
                CSV
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className=\"overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border border-white/[0.07]\">
            <table className=\"w-full min-w-[560px] text-left text-sm\">
              <thead>
                <tr className=\"border-b border-white/[0.07] bg-white/[0.03] text-[11px] tracking-widest text-muted-foreground uppercase\">
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 font-semibold\">Account</th>
                  <th className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 font-semibold md:table-cell\">
                    Member since
                  </th>
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 font-semibold\">Role</th>
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold\">
                    Actions
                  </th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {users === undefined &&
                  [0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
                    <tr key={i}>
                      <td colSpan={4} className=\"px-4 py-3\">
                        <div className=\"h-6 animate-pulse rounded bg-white/[0.05]\" />
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                {filteredUsers.map((user) => {
                  const isSelf = user._id === me?._id;
                  const name =
                    user.name ??
                    (user.isAnonymous ? \"Guest listener\" : \"Unnamed listener\");
                  const email = user.email ?? \"Anonymous sign-in\";
                  return (
                    <tr
                      key={user._id}
                      className=\"border-b border-white/[0.04] last:border-0\"
                    >
                      <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">
                        <div className=\"flex items-center gap-3\">
                          {user.image ? (
                            <img
                              src={user.image}
                              alt=\"\"
                              className=\"size-9 shrink-0 rounded-full object-cover ring-1 ring-white/10\"
                              loading=\"lazy\"
                            />
                          ) : (
                            <span className=\"flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-[rgb(var(--tint)_/_0.14)] text-xs font-bold text-[rgb(var(--tint))]\">
                              {name
                                .split(/\\s+/)
                                .map((w) => w[0])
                                .join(\"\")
                                .slice(0, 2)
                                .toUpperCase()}
                            </span>
                          )}
                          <div className=\"min-w-0\">
                            <p className=\"truncate font-medium\">
                              {name}
                              {isSelf && (
                                <span className=\"ml-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">
                                  (you)
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </p>
                            <p className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground\">
                              {email}
                              {user.providers?.includes(\"github\") && (
                                <button
                                  type=\"button\"
                                  onClick={() =>
                                    openGithub(user._id, user.githubId, name)
                                  }
                                  title={`GitHub account${user.githubId ? ` #${user.githubId}` : \"\"} — click for profile`}
                                  className=\"inline-flex items-center rounded bg-white/[0.07] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold transition-colors hover:bg-white/[0.15] hover:text-foreground\"
                                >
                                  <svg
                                    viewBox=\"0 0 24 24\"
                                    className=\"mr-1 size-3 fill-current\"
                                    aria-hidden=\"true\"
                                  >
                                    <path d=\"M12 .5C5.65.5.5 5.65.5 12c0 5.08 3.29 9.39 7.86 10.91.58.11.79-.25.79-.55v-2.17c-3.2.7-3.87-1.36-3.87-1.36-.52-1.33-1.28-1.69-1.28-1.69-1.04-.71.08-.7.08-.7 1.15.08 1.76 1.19 1.76 1.19 1.03 1.76 2.7 1.25 3.36.96.1-.75.4-1.25.72-1.54-2.55-.29-5.23-1.28-5.23-5.68 0-1.26.45-2.28 1.19-3.09-.12-.29-.52-1.46.11-3.05 0 0 .97-.31 3.18 1.18a11 11 0 0 1 5.79 0c2.2-1.49 3.17-1.18 3.17-1.18.63 1.59.23 2.76.12 3.05.74.81 1.18 1.83 1.18 3.09 0 4.41-2.69 5.38-5.25 5.67.41.36.78 1.06.78 2.14v3.17c0 .3.21.67.8.55A11.5 11.5 0 0 0 23.5 12C23.5 5.65 18.35.5 12 .5z\" />
                                  </svg>
                                  GitHub
                                </button>
                              )}
                              {user.providers?.includes(\"google\") && (
                                <span className=\"inline-flex items-center rounded bg-white/[0.07] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold\">
                                  Google
                                </span>
                              )}
                              {user.providers?.includes(\"email-otp\") && (
                                <span className=\"inline-flex items-center rounded bg-white/[0.07] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold\">
                                  Email
                                </span>
                              )}
                              {user.providers?.includes(\"anonymous\") && (
                                <span className=\"inline-flex items-center rounded bg-white/[0.07] px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold\">
                                  Guest
                                </span>
                              )}
                            </p>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground md:table-cell\">
                        {formatDate(user._creationTime)}
                      </td>
                      <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">
                        {user.role === \"admin\" ? (
                          <Badge className=\"bg-[rgb(var(--tint)_/_0.16)] text-[rgb(var(--tint))] ring-1 ring-[rgb(var(--tint)_/_0.3)]\">
                            Admin
                          </Badge>
                        ) : (
                          <Badge
                            variant=\"secondary\"
                            className=\"bg-white/[0.05] text-muted-foreground\"
                          >
                            Standard
                          </Badge>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">
                        <div className=\"flex items-center justify-end gap-2\">
                          {!isSelf && (
                            <>
                              {user.role === \"admin\" ? (
                                <button
                                  type=\"button\"
                                  onClick={() =>
                                    handleSetRole(user._id, name, \"user\")
                                  }
                                  className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-white/[0.06] hover:text-foreground\"
                                >
                                  <ArrowDownToLine className=\"size-3.5\" />
                                  Demote
                                </button>
                              ) : (
                                <button
                                  type=\"button\"
                                  onClick={() =>
                                    handleSetRole(user._id, name, \"admin\")
                                  }
                                  className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-white/[0.06] hover:text-foreground\"
                                >
                                  <ArrowUpFromLine className=\"size-3.5\" />
                                  Make admin
                                </button>
                              )}
                              <ConfirmButton
                                label=\"Clear history\"
                                confirmLabel=\"Clear all plays?\"
                                onConfirm={() =>
                                  handleClearHistory(user._id, name)
                                }
                              />
                              <ConfirmButton
                                label=\"Delete\"
                                onConfirm={() =>
                                  handleDeleteUser(user._id, name)
                                }
                              />
                            </>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value=\"likes\" className=\"mt-6 flex flex-col gap-6\">
          {/* per-user activity rollup */}
          <div className=\"overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border border-white/[0.07]\">
            <table className=\"w-full min-w-[560px] text-left text-sm\">
              <thead>
                <tr className=\"border-b border-white/[0.07] bg-white/[0.03] text-[11px] tracking-widest text-muted-foreground uppercase\">
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 font-semibold\">Listener</th>
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 font-semibold\">Likes</th>
                  <th className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 font-semibold sm:table-cell\">
                    Playlists
                  </th>
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 font-semibold\">Plays</th>
                  <th className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 font-semibold md:table-cell\">
                    Last played
                  </th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {activity === undefined &&
                  [0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
                    <tr key={i}>
                      <td colSpan={5} className=\"px-4 py-3\">
                        <div className=\"h-6 animate-pulse rounded bg-white/[0.05]\" />
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                {activity?.map((row) => (
                  <tr
                    key={row._id}
                    className=\"border-b border-white/[0.04] last:border-0\"
                  >
                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 font-medium\">
                      {row.name ?? row.email ?? \"Anonymous\"}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground tabular-nums\">
                      {row.likes}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground tabular-nums sm:table-cell\">
                      {row.playlists}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground tabular-nums\">
                      {row.plays}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground md:table-cell\">
                      {row.lastPlayedAt ? formatDate(row.lastPlayedAt) : \"—\"}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          {/* every liked song across the deployment */}
          <div className=\"overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border border-white/[0.07]\">
            <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-white/[0.07] bg-white/[0.03] px-4 py-3\">
              <Heart className=\"size-4 text-[rgb(var(--tint))]\" />
              <p className=\"text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-widest text-muted-foreground uppercase\">
                All liked songs · {allLikes?.length ?? \"…\"}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className=\"max-h-[420px] overflow-y-auto\">
              <table className=\"w-full min-w-[560px] text-left text-sm\">
                <tbody>
                  {allLikes === undefined &&
                    [0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
                      <tr key={i}>
                        <td colSpan={4} className=\"px-4 py-3\">
                          <div className=\"h-10 animate-pulse rounded bg-white/[0.05]\" />
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  {allLikes?.map((like) => (
                    <tr
                      key={like._id}
                      className=\"border-b border-white/[0.04] last:border-0\"
                    >
                      <td className=\"w-12 px-4 py-2\">
                        {like.coverUrl ? (
                          <img
                            src={like.coverUrl}
                            alt=\"\"
                            loading=\"lazy\"
                            className=\"size-10 rounded-md object-cover\"
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <span className=\"flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-white/[0.06]\">
                            <Heart className=\"size-4 text-muted-foreground/50\" />
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className=\"px-2 py-2\">
                        <p className=\"truncate font-medium\">{like.title}</p>
                        <p className=\"truncate text-xs text-muted-foreground\">
                          {like.artistName}
                        </p>
                      </td>
                      <td className=\"px-4 py-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">
                        {like.ownerName ?? like.ownerEmail ?? \"Unknown\"}
                      </td>
                      <td className=\"hidden px-4 py-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground md:table-cell\">
                        {formatDate(like.createdAt)}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                  {allLikes !== undefined && allLikes.length === 0 && (
                    <tr>
                      <td
                        colSpan={4}
                        className=\"px-4 py-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground\"
                      >
                        No liked songs yet.
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* deployment-wide most played */}
          <div className=\"rounded-2xl border border-white/[0.07] bg-white/[0.03] p-5\">
            <p className=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold\">
              <TrendingUp className=\"size-4 text-[rgb(var(--tint))]\" />
              Most played across the deployment
            </p>
            <div className=\"mt-3 flex flex-col gap-1.5\">
              {topTracks === undefined && (
                <div className=\"h-16 animate-pulse rounded bg-white/[0.05]\" />
              )}
              {topTracks?.map((t, i) => (
                <div
                  key={t.trackId}
                  className=\"flex items-center gap-3 text-sm\"
                >
                  <span className=\"w-6 text-right text-xs tabular-nums text-muted-foreground\">
                    {i + 1}
                  </span>
                  {/* The song's TITLE, not its id. This list used to render the
                      raw trackId, which told an admin nothing they could act
                      on. */}
                  <span className=\"min-w-0 flex-1 truncate\">
                    {t.title ?? (
                      // No snapshot on any of this track's rows — an older
                      // play, recorded before titles were stored. Showing the
                      // id in muted type is honest; inventing a name is not.
                      <span
                        className=\"font-mono text-xs text-muted-foreground/70\"
                        title=\"No title was recorded for this track\"
                      >
                        {t.trackId} · title not recorded
                      </span>
                    )}
                    {t.title && t.artistName ? (
                      <span className=\"text-muted-foreground\">
                        {\" — \"}
                        {t.artistName}
                      </span>
                    ) : null}
                  </span>
                  <span className=\"text-xs tabular-nums text-muted-foreground\">
                    {t.plays} plays · {t.listeners} listener
                    {t.listeners === 1 ? \"\" : \"s\"}
                  </span>
                </div>
              ))}
              {topTracks !== undefined && topTracks.length === 0 && (
                <p className=\"text-sm text-muted-foreground\">
                  No listening history yet.
                </p>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value=\"playlists\" className=\"mt-6 flex flex-col gap-3\">
          <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row sm:items-center\">
            <input
              value={playlistQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setPlaylistQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder=\"Search playlist, owner or id…\"
              aria-label=\"Search playlists\"
              className=\"h-10 w-full rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04] px-4 text-sm outline-none placeholder:text-muted-foreground/70 focus:border-[rgb(var(--tint)_/_0.5)] sm:max-w-xs\"
            />
            <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5\">
              <select
                value={playlistSort}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setPlaylistSort(e.target.value as typeof playlistSort)
                }
                aria-label=\"Sort playlists\"
                className=\"rounded-full border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04] px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-muted-foreground outline-none\"
              >
                <option value=\"newest\">Newest</option>
                <option value=\"largest\">Most tracks</option>
                <option value=\"name\">Name</option>
              </select>
              <span className=\"text-[11px] text-muted-foreground\">
                {filteredPlaylists.length} of {playlists?.length ?? 0}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className=\"overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border border-white/[0.07]\">
            <table className=\"w-full min-w-[560px] text-left text-sm\">
              <thead>
                <tr className=\"border-b border-white/[0.07] bg-white/[0.03] text-[11px] tracking-widest text-muted-foreground uppercase\">
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 font-semibold\">Playlist</th>
                  <th className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 font-semibold sm:table-cell\">
                    Owner
                  </th>
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 font-semibold\">Tracks</th>
                  <th className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 font-semibold md:table-cell\">
                    Created
                  </th>
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold\">
                    Actions
                  </th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {playlists === undefined &&
                  [0, 1].map((i) => (
                    <tr key={i}>
                      <td colSpan={5} className=\"px-4 py-3\">
                        <div className=\"h-6 animate-pulse rounded bg-white/[0.05]\" />
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                {filteredPlaylists.map((playlist) => (
                  <tr
                    key={playlist._id}
                    className=\"border-b border-white/[0.04] last:border-0\"
                  >
                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 font-medium\">{playlist.name}</td>
                    <td className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground sm:table-cell\">
                      {playlist.ownerName ?? playlist.ownerEmail ?? \"Unknown\"}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground\">
                      {playlist.trackCount}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground md:table-cell\">
                      {formatDate(playlist._creationTime)}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">
                      <div className=\"flex items-center justify-end gap-2\">
                        {renaming && renaming.id === playlist._id ? (
                          <form
                            onSubmit={(e) => {
                              e.preventDefault();
                              if (renaming.name.trim())
                                handleRenamePlaylist(
                                  renaming.id,
                                  renaming.name,
                                );
                            }}
                            className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5\"
                          >
                            <input
                              autoFocus
                              value={renaming?.name ?? \"\"}
                              onChange={(e) =>
                                setRenaming({
                                  id: playlist._id,
                                  name: e.target.value,
                                })
                              }
                              className=\"w-40 rounded-full border border-white/15 bg-white/[0.05] px-3 py-1.5 text-xs outline-none focus:border-[rgb(var(--tint))]\"
                            />
                            <button
                              type=\"submit\"
                              className=\"rounded-full bg-[rgb(var(--tint))] px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-black\"
                            >
                              Save
                            </button>
                            <button
                              type=\"button\"
                              onClick={() => setRenaming(null)}
                              className=\"rounded-full border border-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground\"
                            >
                              Cancel
                            </button>
                          </form>
                        ) : (
                          <>
                            <button
                              type=\"button\"
                              aria-label={`Inspect the tracks in ${playlist.name}`}
                              title=\"See what's in this playlist\"
                              onClick={() => setInspecting(playlist._id)}
                              className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-white/[0.06] hover:text-foreground\"
                            >
                              <Eye className=\"size-3.5\" />
                              Inspect
                            </button>

                            <button
                              type=\"button\"
                              aria-label={`Rename ${playlist.name}`}
                              title=\"Rename\"
                              onClick={() =>
                                setRenaming({
                                  id: playlist._id,
                                  name: playlist.name,
                                })
                              }
                              className=\"flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-white/10 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-white/[0.06] hover:text-foreground\"
                            >
                              <Pencil className=\"size-3.5\" />
                              Rename
                            </button>
                            <ConfirmButton
                              label=\"Delete\"
                              confirmLabel=\"Delete playlist?\"
                              onConfirm={() =>
                                handleDeletePlaylist(playlist._id)
                              }
                            />
                          </>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
                {playlists !== undefined && playlists.length === 0 && (
                  <tr>
                    <td
                      colSpan={5}
                      className=\"px-4 py-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground\"
                    >
                      No playlists exist yet.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          <PlaylistInspector
            detail={inspected}
            onClose={() => setInspecting(null)}
          />
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value=\"insights\" className=\"mt-6 flex flex-col gap-6\">
          <div className=\"grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3\">
            <StatCard
              icon={Activity}
              label=\"Plays · last 14 days\"
              value={listeningStats?.totalPlays ?? 0}
            />
            <StatCard
              icon={BarChart3}
              label=\"Unique tracks · 14 days\"
              value={listeningStats?.uniqueTracks ?? 0}
            />
            <StatCard
              icon={Clock3}
              label=\"Peak listening hour\"
              value={
                listeningStats?.peakHour == null
                  ? \"—\"
                  : `${String(listeningStats.peakHour).padStart(2, \"0\")}:00`
              }
            />
          </div>

          <div className=\"rounded-2xl border border-white/[0.07] bg-white/[0.03] p-5\">
            <p className=\"text-sm font-semibold\">
              Plays per day — last 14 days
            </p>
            {listeningStats === undefined ? (
              <div className=\"mt-3 h-32 animate-pulse rounded bg-white/[0.05]\" />
            ) : listeningStats.totalPlays === 0 ? (
              <div className=\"mt-3 flex h-32 flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-dashed border-white/[0.08] bg-black/10 px-4 text-center\">
                <BarChart3 className=\"size-6 text-muted-foreground/45\" />
                <p className=\"mt-2 text-sm font-semibold\">
                  No listening activity yet
                </p>
                <p className=\"mt-1 max-w-sm text-xs text-muted-foreground\">
                  Plays from the last 14 days will appear here as people listen.
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className=\"mt-4 flex h-32 items-end gap-1.5\">
                {listeningStats.daily.map((d) => {
                  const max = Math.max(
                    1,
                    ...listeningStats.daily.map((x) => x.plays),
                  );
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={d.day}
                      className=\"group relative z-10 flex h-full min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col items-center gap-1 hover:z-20\"
                    >
                      <div
                        className=\"relative w-full rounded-t bg-[rgb(var(--tint)_/_0.55)] transition-colors group-hover:bg-[rgb(var(--tint))]\"
                        style={{
                          height: `${Math.max(3, (d.plays / max) * 100)}%`,
                        }}
                        aria-label={`${d.label}: ${d.plays} plays`}
                      >
                        <span className=\"pointer-events-none absolute bottom-[calc(100%+8px)] left-1/2 z-20 hidden w-max -translate-x-1/2 rounded-lg border border-white/10 bg-[#12151d]/95 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left text-[10px] text-foreground shadow-xl backdrop-blur-xl group-hover:block\">
                          <span className=\"block font-semibold\">{d.label}</span>
                          <span className=\"block text-muted-foreground\">
                            {d.plays} {d.plays === 1 ? \"play\" : \"plays\"}
                          </span>
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <span className=\"w-full truncate text-center text-[9px] text-muted-foreground\">
                        {d.label.split(\" \")[1]}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value=\"visits\" className=\"mt-6\">
          <VisitAnalytics
            summary={visitSummary}
            days={visitDays}
            onDaysChange={setVisitDays}
          />
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value=\"rooms\" className=\"mt-6\">
          <div className=\"overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border border-white/[0.07]\">
            <table className=\"w-full min-w-[560px] text-left text-sm\">
              <thead>
                <tr className=\"border-b border-white/[0.07] bg-white/[0.03] text-[11px] tracking-widest text-muted-foreground uppercase\">
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 font-semibold\">Room</th>
                  <th className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 font-semibold sm:table-cell\">
                    Host
                  </th>
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 font-semibold\">Members</th>
                  <th className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 font-semibold md:table-cell\">
                    Now playing
                  </th>
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold\">
                    Actions
                  </th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {rooms === undefined &&
                  [0, 1].map((i) => (
                    <tr key={i}>
                      <td colSpan={5} className=\"px-4 py-3\">
                        <div className=\"h-6 animate-pulse rounded bg-white/[0.05]\" />
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                {rooms?.map((room) => (
                  <tr
                    key={room._id}
                    className=\"border-b border-white/[0.04] last:border-0\"
                  >
                    <td className=\"flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 font-medium\">
                      <Radio className=\"size-4 text-[rgb(var(--tint))]\" />
                      {room.name}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground sm:table-cell\">
                      {room.hostName}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground tabular-nums\">
                      {room.memberCount}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"hidden max-w-[220px] truncate px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground md:table-cell\">
                      {room.nowPlaying ?? \"—\"}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">
                      <div className=\"flex justify-end\">
                        <ConfirmButton
                          label=\"Remove\"
                          confirmLabel=\"Remove room?\"
                          onConfirm={() => handleDeleteRoom(room._id)}
                        />
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
                {rooms !== undefined && rooms.length === 0 && (
                  <tr>
                    <td
                      colSpan={5}
                      className=\"px-4 py-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground\"
                    >
                      No listening rooms exist yet.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value=\"imports\" className=\"mt-6\">
          <div className=\"overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border border-white/[0.07]\">
            <table className=\"w-full min-w-[560px] text-left text-sm\">
              <thead>
                <tr className=\"border-b border-white/[0.07] bg-white/[0.03] text-[11px] tracking-widest text-muted-foreground uppercase\">
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 font-semibold\">Playlist</th>
                  <th className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 font-semibold sm:table-cell\">
                    Origin
                  </th>
                  <th className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 font-semibold sm:table-cell\">
                    Owner
                  </th>
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 font-semibold\">Tracks</th>
                  <th className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 font-semibold md:table-cell\">
                    Imported
                  </th>
                  <th className=\"px-4 py-3 text-right font-semibold\">
                    Actions
                  </th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {imports === undefined &&
                  [0, 1].map((i) => (
                    <tr key={i}>
                      <td colSpan={6} className=\"px-4 py-3\">
                        <div className=\"h-6 animate-pulse rounded bg-white/[0.05]\" />
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                {imports?.map((imp) => (
                  <tr
                    key={imp._id}
                    className=\"border-b border-white/[0.04] last:border-0\"
                  >
                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 font-medium\">{imp.name}</td>
                    <td className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground sm:table-cell\">
                      {imp.origin}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground sm:table-cell\">
                      {imp.ownerName}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground tabular-nums\">
                      {imp.trackCount}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"hidden px-4 py-3 text-muted-foreground md:table-cell\">
                      {formatDate(imp.createdAt)}
                    </td>
                    <td className=\"px-4 py-3\">
                      <div className=\"flex justify-end\">
                        <ConfirmButton
                          label=\"Delete\"
                          confirmLabel=\"Delete import?\"
                          onConfirm={() => handleDeleteImport(imp._id)}
                        />
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
                {imports !== undefined && imports.length === 0 && (
                  <tr>
                    <td
                      colSpan={6}
                      className=\"px-4 py-10 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground\"
                    >
                      No imported playlists yet.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </TabsContent>
      </Tabs>

      {/* GitHub profile popover */}
      {gh && (
        <div
          className=\"fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/60 p-4\"
          onClick={() => setGh(null)}
        >
          <div
            className=\"w-full max-w-sm rounded-2xl border border-white/10 bg-[#141419] p-6 shadow-2xl\"
            onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <p className=\"text-sm font-semibold\">GitHub account — {gh.name}</p>
            {gh.loading && (
              <div className=\"mt-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground\">
                <Loader2 className=\"size-4 animate-spin\" /> Fetching profile…
              </div>
            )}
            {!gh.loading && gh.data && (
              <div className=\"mt-4 flex items-start gap-4\">
                {gh.data.avatarUrl && (
                  <img
                    src={gh.data.avatarUrl}
                    alt=\"\"
                    className=\"size-14 rounded-full ring-1 ring-white/10\"
                    loading=\"lazy\"
                  />
                )}
                <div className=\"min-w-0 text-sm\">
                  <p className=\"truncate font-medium\">
                    {gh.data.name ?? gh.data.login}
                  </p>
                  <a
                    href={gh.data.htmlUrl ?? undefined}
                    target=\"_blank\"
                    rel=\"noreferrer\"
                    className=\"text-xs text-[rgb(var(--tint))] hover:underline\"
                  >
                    @{gh.data.login} — open on GitHub
                  </a>
                  <p className=\"mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">
                    {gh.data.publicRepos} public repos
                  </p>
                  {gh.data.createdAt && (
                    <p className=\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\">
                      Joined GitHub {new Date(gh.data.createdAt).getFullYear()}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            {!gh.loading && !gh.data && (
              <p className=\"mt-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground\">
                Couldn&apos;t load this GitHub profile (account may be private
                or deleted).
              </p>
            )}
            <button
              type=\"button\"
              onClick={() => setGh(null)}
              className=\"mt-5 w-full rounded-full border border-white/10 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors hover:bg-white/[0.06]\"
            >
              Close
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

        {value}
      </p>
      <p className=\"mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground\">{label}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

/**
 * Resolve a direct audio stream URL for a YouTube video via the Convex
 * backend (vanilla client — usable outside React). Used only when an audio
 * effect needs the stream on the <audio> element.
 *
 * The URL is routed through our CORS proxy (/audio-proxy on the Convex
 * site): stream hosts send no CORS headers, so without the proxy the
 * <audio crossOrigin=\"anonymous\"> element can't load them and every effect
 * silently becomes a no-op. Returns null on failure.
 */
let ytResolveClient: ConvexClient | null = null;
let ytSearchClient: ConvexClient | null = null;
const convexSiteUrl = (
  (import.meta.env.VITE_CONVEX_URL as string) ?? \"\"
).replace(\".cloud\", \".site\");
const audioProxyBase = `${convexSiteUrl}/audio-proxy`;

/**
 * The first NON-NULL value from several promises.
 *
 * Deliberately NOT `Promise.race`: a race settles on the first to SETTLE, and
 * here the first to settle is usually the fast FAILURE — the Convex resolver
 * answering \"no stream\" in ~230ms. That would discard Piped's real answer a
 * moment later and report a false outage. This resolves on the first SUCCESS,
 * and reports null only once every branch has produced null.
 */
function firstResolved<T>(promises: Promise<T | null>[]): Promise<T | null> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    let pending = promises.length;
    let settled = false;
    for (const promise of promises) {
      promise
        .then((value) => {
          if (settled || value == null) return;
          settled = true;
          resolve(value);
        })
        .catch(() => undefined)
        .finally(() => {
          if (settled) return;
          if (--pending === 0) {
            settled = true;
            resolve(null);
          }
        });
    }
  });
}
const resolveYoutubeStream = async (
  videoId: string,
): Promise<string | null> => {
  // NOTE: this used to start the YouTube IFrame here too — \"so the listener
  // hears the dry track while the direct stream is being resolved\". That was
  // the redundant download described in `loadTrack`: the IFrame and the deck
  // then pulled the same song from two unrelated CDNs at the same moment, and
  // the live deck lost the bandwidth and stalled. Whoever resolves the stream
  // decides which engine plays it, and only ONE engine may ever play it.
  //
  // `loadTrack` is now the single place that chooses an engine, so this
  // resolver is purely a URL lookup and starts no playback at all.
  // NOTE: there used to be a browser-side InnerTube attempt here, spoofing an
  // ANDROID_VR / TVHTML5 client to resolve a direct googlevideo URL from the
  // USER's IP so audio effects would survive YouTube's datacenter bot-check.
  // It has been removed because it CANNOT work from a browser, for two
  // structural reasons rather than fixable ones:
  //
  //   1. `https://www.youtube.com/youtubei/v1/player` returns no
  //      `Access-Control-Allow-Origin` at all, and answers the preflight with
  //      403. Verified directly against the live endpoint rather than assumed:
  //      an OPTIONS request carrying an Origin returns 403 and not one
  //      `access-control-*` header.
  //   2. `User-Agent` is a FORBIDDEN header name, so `fetch` silently dropped
  //      the one header the client-spoofing depended on. It never reached the
  //      network wearing the identity it was pretending to have.
  //
  // So it never returned a URL, and all it actually produced was two CORS
  // errors in the console on every single play, alongside a comment claiming
  // resolution ran from the user's IP. The two resolvers below are the real
  // paths, and they are unchanged.
  const viaConvex = async (): Promise<string | null> => {
    try {
      ytResolveClient =
        ytResolveClient ??
        new ConvexClient(import.meta.env.VITE_CONVEX_URL as string);
      // Network-aware quality: prefer a lighter stream when the connection is poor.
      type NetInfo = { effectiveType?: string };
      const net = (navigator as Navigator & { connection?: NetInfo })
        .connection;
      const slow =
        net?.effectiveType === \"2g\" ||
        net?.effectiveType === \"slow-2g\" ||
        net?.effectiveType === \"3g\";
      const res = await ytResolveClient.action(api.youtube.resolveStream, {
        videoId,
        quality: slow ? \"low\" : \"high\",
      });
      if (res?.url) return `${audioProxyBase}?u=${encodeURIComponent(res.url)}`;
      return null;
    } catch {
      // Any Convex failure is expected and falls through to Piped.
      return null;
    }
  };

  // Both resolvers, AT ONCE.
  //
  // They used to run in sequence: the Convex action first, and the
  // browser-side Piped path only when it returned null. Convex runs on a
  // datacenter IP and meets YouTube's bot-check, so it returns null for MOST
  // tracks — measured live at 4 of 5. In sequence that cost `convex + piped`
  // (501-693ms measured on exactly those tracks) before the deck even knew it
  // had no stream, and the listener heard nothing for the whole of it. Racing
  // them takes Convex's latency out of the failure path entirely — it becomes
  // `max(convex, piped)`, 282-389ms measured — and changes nothing when Convex
  // succeeds.
  //
  // The loser is ABORTED rather than left running, so a Convex success does not
  // spend one of the two surviving Piped instances' requests for nothing.
  const aborter = new AbortController();

  // Browser-side resolution via public Piped instances. These resolve from the
  // USER's IP, so unlike the Convex resolver they are not met with YouTube's
  // datacenter bot-check. The implementation lives in `lib/pipedResolve` so the
  // download path resolves exactly the same way — while it was inline here,
  // playback worked and downloading did not, for the same track at once.
  // NOTE: `resolveViaPiped` returns `{ url, failures }`, NOT a bare string.
  // This call site must read `.url` — an `if (piped)` truthiness check would be
  // true even when resolution failed, and would hand the caller an object where
  // a URL string belongs.
  const viaPiped = resolveViaPiped(videoId, aborter.signal)
    .then((piped) => piped.url)
    .catch(() => null);

  const url = await firstResolved([viaConvex(), viaPiped]);
  // Cancel whichever branch is still in flight. Convex's action cannot be
  // aborted, but the Piped request can, and that is the one worth saving.
  aborter.abort();
  return url;
};

/**
 * Resolve, but never wait forever.
 *
 * `ytResolve` talks to a Convex action and then to a list of public Piped
 * instances. Any of those can hang — and while it does, the track sits at
 * `isBuffering: true` with position 0.0 and no sound, because the IFrame that
 * could have played it has not been started yet. Resolution therefore loses a
 * race against a short timer: the YouTube player is the only audio source that
 * has ever reliably worked, so it must never be gated behind a best-effort
 * lookup.
 */
const RESOLVE_RACE_MS = 1_200;

function resolveWithDeadline(videoId: string): Promise<string | null> {
  return Promise.race([
    ytResolve(videoId),
    new Promise<null>((r) => setTimeout(() => r(null), RESOLVE_RACE_MS)),
  ]);
}

const ytResolveCache = new Map<string, Promise<string | null>>();

/**
 * Which tracks have a usable local copy: videoId -> `blob:` url, or `null`
 * once we have looked and found none.
 *
 * `undefined` means \"not looked yet\". That third state matters: without it,
 * every play of an undownloaded track would re-stat the OPFS directory, and
 * the directory grows with every download.
 */
const localAudioUrls = new Map<string, string | null>();

/**
 * Look up a downloaded copy, remember the answer, and resolve to it.
 *
 * Returns the `blob:` url when the track is on disk and `null` when it is not,
 * so `ytResolve` can await the answer instead of guessing. It also de-duplicates
 * concurrent lookups for the same track, so a prewarm and a play landing at the
 * same moment do not both stat the directory.
 */
const localAudioPending = new Map<string, Promise<string | null>>();

function primeLocalAudio(videoId: string): Promise<string | null> {
  const existing = localAudioPending.get(videoId);
  if (existing) return existing;
  const pending = localAudioUrl(videoId)
    .then((url) => {
      localAudioUrls.set(videoId, url);
      return url;
    })
    .catch(() => {
      localAudioUrls.set(videoId, null);
      return null;
    })
    .finally(() => {
      localAudioPending.delete(videoId);
    });
  localAudioPending.set(videoId, pending);
  return pending;
}

/**
 * Called when a download finishes so the next play of that track goes straight
 * to disk instead of resolving a stream it already has.
 */
export function noteTrackDownloaded(videoId: string): void {
  localAudioUrls.delete(videoId);
  // The cached network URL, if any, is now wrong to prefer.
  ytResolveCache.delete(videoId);
  void primeLocalAudio(videoId);
}

/** Called when a download is deleted, so the track streams again. */
export function noteTrackRemoved(videoId: string): void {
  localAudioUrls.set(videoId, null);
  ytResolveCache.delete(videoId);
}

/** Resolve once per track, then reuse the stream while EQ/effects are adjusted. */
const ytResolve = (videoId: string): Promise<string | null> => {
  // A downloaded copy wins, always.
  //
  // This check comes BEFORE the network on purpose. `resolveYoutubeStream`
  // talks to Convex and can take seconds; when the track is already on disk
  // that is pure dead time, and offline it fails outright. Checking here —
  // rather than in `loadTrack` — means every caller benefits at once:
  // `prepareIncoming` (gapless/crossfade), the EQ re-resolve path, and the
  // rescue path all route through `ytResolve`.
  const local = localAudioUrls.get(videoId);
  if (local !== undefined) {
    // `null` records \"we looked and there is nothing\", so a track that is not
    // downloaded does not re-hit OPFS on every single play.
    return Promise.resolve(local);
  }
  // Never primed yet. This is the NORMAL case on a first play, because
  // `primeLocalAudio` is fire-and-forget and the OPFS stat has not necessarily
  // landed by the time we get here. Falling through to the network here meant a
  // downloaded track ignored its own copy on first play and failed outright
  // offline. So the lookup is awaited rather than assumed.
  return primeLocalAudio(videoId).then((url) => {
    if (url) return url;
    // Genuinely not on disk: continue down the normal resolution path.
    const cached = getCachedStream(videoId);
    if (cached) return cached;
    const existing = ytResolveCache.get(videoId);
    if (existing) return existing;
    const pending = resolveYoutubeStream(videoId).then((resolved) => {
      if (resolved) cacheStream(videoId, resolved);
      else ytResolveCache.delete(videoId);
      return resolved;
    });
    ytResolveCache.set(videoId, pending);
    return pending;
  });
};

/** Start EQ stream resolution before the user changes a slider. */
export function prewarmYoutubeStream(videoId: string): void {
  void ytResolve(videoId);
}

export type RepeatMode = \"off\" | \"all\" | \"one\";
/** Re-exported so consumers import the level from the store, as with the rest. */
export type { AnimationLevel };

let likeMutation: LikeMutation | null = null;

export interface RemoteLikeSnapshot {
  title: string;
  artistName: string;
  coverUrl: string;
  duration: number;
  youtubeId?: string;
  source: string;
}

export interface LikeMutation {
  (trackId: string, remote?: RemoteLikeSnapshot): Promise<boolean>;
}

/** How a play was started. Mirrors the engine's `PlaySignal[\"origin\"]`. */
export type PlayOrigin = \"user\" | \"autoplay\" | \"recommendation\" | \"radio\";

/**
 * Metadata handed to the play recorder alongside the id.
 *
 * The recorder used to re-read `current` from the store to build this, but
 * `loadTrack` calls it BEFORE committing the new track — so the lookup always
 * returned the PREVIOUS song and every remote history row was stored with no
 * title, artist or cover. Without an artist the recommendation engine has no
 * affinity signal at all, so the snapshot is passed in explicitly now.
 */
export interface HistoryMeta {
  source?: string;
  title?: string;
  artistName?: string;
  coverUrl?: string;
  duration?: number;
  albumName?: string;
  origin?: PlayOrigin;
}

export type HistoryRecorder = (trackId: string, meta?: HistoryMeta) => void;

/**
 * Bridge to the recommendation backend (wired to Convex in AppShell).
 *
 * Every method is optional and every call site is wrapped in try/catch: signal
 * recording is observability, and losing it must never interrupt playback.
 */
export interface SignalBridge {
  /**
   * Record that a play started. Returns (or resolves to) the server-side
   * `startedAt` for that exact play, which is what lets the matching
   * engagement outcome be attached to the right row.
   */
  playStart(args: {
    trackId: string;
    meta: HistoryMeta;
  }): number | Promise<number> | void;
  /** The play ended, or was skipped part-way. */
  engage(args: {
    trackId: string;
    startedAt: number;
    completion: number;
    listenedSeconds: number;
    skipped: boolean;
  }): void;
  /** The listener deleted an automatically generated track from the queue. */
  removal(args: { trackId: string }): void;
  /** Something happened to a recommendation (played / liked / queued). */
  feedback(args: {
    trackId: string;
    outcome: string;
    section?: string;
    reason?: string;
    origin?: PlayOrigin;
    title?: string;
    artistName?: string;
    albumName?: string;
  }): void;
}

let signalBridge: SignalBridge | null = null;

/** Pending outcome for the track that is currently playing. */
interface EngagementSession {
  trackId: string;
  origin: PlayOrigin;
  /** Seconds of the track already behind us when playback began (resume). */
  startOffset: number;
  /** Server-side id of the play row, filled in once the start is confirmed. */
  startedAt: number;
}

let engagement: EngagementSession | null = null;
/** Origin applied to the NEXT loadTrack call. */
let pendingOrigin: PlayOrigin = \"user\";

/**
 * Close out the current play: how much was heard, and whether it was a skip.
 *
 * Called when the track ends (a completion) and when another track takes over
 * (a skip). Clearing `engagement` first means the second call is a no-op, so a
 * track that ends and then advances cannot be recorded twice.
 */
function settleEngagement(reason: \"ended\" | \"changed\"): void {
  const session = engagement;
  if (!session) return;
  engagement = null;

  const bridge = signalBridge;
  if (!bridge || !session.startedAt) return;

  try {
    const s = usePlayerStore.getState();
    const duration =
      s.current && s.current.id === session.trackId
        ? s.current.duration
        : s.duration;
    const position =
      s.current && s.current.id === session.trackId
        ? s.currentTime
        : s.currentTime;
    const heard = Math.max(0, position - session.startOffset);
    if (duration <= 0) return;
    const completion = Math.min(1, heard / duration);
    // \"ended\" is authoritative; \"changed\" is a skip unless the listener had
    // already heard most of the track, in which case it is a weak signal at
    // most. A single skip must never look like a rejection of the whole artist.
    const skipped = reason === \"changed\" && completion < 0.7;
    bridge.engage({
      trackId: session.trackId,
